2285 — lever pullover

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2285 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A machine pullover that sweeps the handles toward you. Works the lats with a long range of motion.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Adjust the seat and handles of the leverage machine to a comfortable position. Sit on the machine with your back against the pad and grasp the handles with an overhand grip.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2285.json#/instructions/0, 2285.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Sit on the machine with your back against the pad and grasp the handles with an overhand grip.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2285.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

For an otherwise unspecified seated setup, place the feet in a stable comfortable support position; preserve any source-defined footrests, pedals, leg pads or floor contact.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 2285.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Adjust the seat and handles of the leverage machine to a comfortable position. 2285.json#/instructions/0
  2. Sit on the machine with your back against the pad and grasp the handles with an overhand grip. 2285.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your arms slightly bent and your core engaged. 2285.json#/instructions/2
  4. Slowly pull the handles towards your chest, squeezing your lats. 2285.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the peak contraction, then slowly return to the starting position. 2285.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 2285.json#/instructions/5

2286 — lever hip extension v. 2

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2286 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A machine hip extension that drives your leg back against resistance. Targets the glutes and hamstrings.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Adjust the machine to fit your body and sit on it with your back against the backrest. Place your feet on the footrests and grip the handles for stability.

2287 — lever alternate leg press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2287 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A machine leg press done one leg at a time, alternating. Builds the quads and glutes.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Adjust the seat and foot platform of the leverage machine to your desired position. Sit on the machine with your back against the backrest and your feet on the foot platform. Place your hands on the handles or sides of the machine for stability.

  4. Push one foot against the foot platform, extending your leg until it is almost fully straight. 2287.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment, then slowly bend your leg and return to the starting position. 2287.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat with the other leg. 2287.json#/instructions/5
  7. Continue alternating legs for the desired number of repetitions. 2287.json#/instructions/6

2289 — lever calf press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2289 after decompression) · Source record

A machine calf press pushing the pad with your toes. Builds the calf muscles.

Proposed staging

2290 — dumbbell bulgarian split squat

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2290 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A single-leg squat with the back foot raised, building leg strength and balance.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand a stride length in front of a bench holding a dumbbell in each hand. Rest the top of your back foot on the bench.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2290.json#/instructions/0, 2290.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Stand a stride length in front of a bench holding a dumbbell in each hand.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2290.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Rest the top of your back foot on the bench.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2290.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Dumbbell pair, Adjustable utility bench. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 2290.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand a stride length in front of a bench holding a dumbbell in each hand. 2290.json#/instructions/0
  2. Rest the top of your back foot on the bench. 2290.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lower straight down until your front thigh is about parallel to the floor. 2290.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your torso upright and your front knee tracking over your toes. 2290.json#/instructions/3
  5. Drive through your front heel to stand back up. 2290.json#/instructions/4
  6. Finish all reps, then switch legs. 2290.json#/instructions/5

2291 — cable wide grip lat pulldown

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 2291 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A pulldown with a wide grip that builds the broad muscles of the upper back.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit at the pulldown station and secure your thighs under the pads. Grab the bar with a wide overhand grip.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 2291.json#/instructions/0, 2291.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Grab the bar with a wide overhand grip.

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Adjustable cable station. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 2291.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit at the pulldown station and secure your thighs under the pads. 2291.json#/instructions/0
  2. Grab the bar with a wide overhand grip. 2291.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lean back slightly and pull the bar down to your upper chest. 2291.json#/instructions/2
  4. Drive your elbows down and back, squeezing your shoulder blades. 2291.json#/instructions/3
  5. Control the bar back up until your arms are fully extended. 2291.json#/instructions/4
  6. Avoid swinging your torso. 2291.json#/instructions/5
